"Before you send" checklist: how every line was sorted, state by state
====================================================================

Read this before relying on the checklist. No lawyer has reviewed it.

Each line the old card showed is now one of three things:
  ACTION      a line on the "Before you send" card. Under each one, "Who does it" says one of:
                THE SYSTEM DOES IT     the builder prints the notice (from the official wording on
                                       file) or hands the document over with the contract, by itself.
                THE SYSTEM CAN SEE IT  a person does it inside Apex and the builder notices.
                PERSON ONLY            nothing in Apex can do or see it. A hand tick, as before.
              A system line ticks itself and cannot be ticked or unticked by hand.
  HANDLED     the contract builder already does it (prints it, counts it, inserts the notice).
  BACKGROUND  context only.
HANDLED and BACKGROUND lines are not on the checklist. They stay readable under "More about {State}".

UNSURE marks a line where the research did not make it clear whether the person has to act.
Those were made ACTION lines worded as a check, or HANDLED with the doubt written down.

How to read a state block:
  - It lists every line the state CAN show. A real contract shows fewer: a pool line shows only on
    a pool job, a door-to-door line only when the sale was made at the customer's home, a notice
    only when the contract amount reaches the state's threshold.
  - The blocks assume no license number is on file. When a number is on file, the line
    "Add your license or registration number" is HANDLED (the number prints in the contract).
  - The blocks assume a cleaning plan that renews by itself and a commercial cleaning template, so
    the cleaning lines show as actions. Otherwise those lines are BACKGROUND.
  - A flagged clause choice ("Look again at clause ...") shows only when that clause is chosen,
    so it is not listed here. It is always an ACTION.
  - The text in parentheses after an action is the law reference shown on the card. It is copied
    from the research. "details" means the research gave no law number for that line.
  - "Old card text" is what the card showed before. It is shortened here (ending in "...");
    on the card the person reads all of it by clicking the law reference or "More about {State}".

General notes (a doubt about a rule every state shares):
  1. Worded as a check: the builder cannot tell which license this job needs.
  2. The research found no state-level license and did not look at cities and counties.
  3. The builder writes contracts in English only. The system does this line only when the sale
     was made in English; otherwise a person must give a translation.
  4. No sentence was written for this notice yet; this is the fallback wording.
  5. The research lists a pool rule by name only. Worded as a check.
  6. The research found no rule, which is not proof that none exists.
  7. The research has no note on a cleaning license for this state.
  8. The clause library holds Florida's tax rules only.
  9. A statutory form prints its own blanks (a date line, a signature line) exactly as the law
     prints them; those are filled by hand.

Totals over all states: 587 ACTION, 357 HANDLED, 404 BACKGROUND.
Of the ACTION lines: 129 the system does, 99 the system can see, 359 person only.
The short version is in scripts/fixtures/state-checklist-summary.txt.
Marked UNSURE: 95 lines with a doubt of their own, plus 294 that point to a general note.

To change a sorting or a sentence: edit scripts/make-state-checklist-data.mjs, then run the three
commands at the top of that file.

################ Alabama (AL) - research status: notice text still needs official copy
On the card: 9 action lines at most. Under "More about Alabama": 14 lines.

  ACTION      Make sure your license or registration covers this job in Alabama (details)
              Portuguese (draft): Confira se a sua licença ou registro vale para este serviço em Alabama
              Who does it: PERSON ONLY
              Old card text: License or registration: HBLB residential home builder license when cost exceeds
              $10,000 (roofing over $2,500); pools over $5,000 via the General Contractors board (which board
              governs unresolved).
              UNSURE: see general note 1 at the top.
              [line: license]

  ACTION      Add your license or registration number in the document settings (details)
              Portuguese (draft): Cadastre o número da sua licença ou registro nas configurações dos documentos
              Who does it: THE SYSTEM CAN SEE IT. On the card it reads: Your license or registration number printed in the contract / O número da sua licença ou registro impresso no contrato
              Old card text: No license or registration number is on file. The contract prints "not provided".
              Add the number in the document settings.
              [line: license_number]

  ACTION      Give the customer the written liability insurance disclosure and get it signed before work starts (34-14A-19)
              Portuguese (draft): Entregue ao cliente a declaração de seguro de responsabilidade por escrito e pegue a assinatura antes de começar o serviço
              Who does it: PERSON ONLY
              Old card text: Alabama requires this notice: Liability insurance disclosure (34-14A-19). The
              exact wording is not loaded yet. Get it from the official source or your attorney and attach it
              before the customer signs. When: Before residential home building starts; may be a provision in
              the original contract or a separate addendum. Format: Insured builder: signed or initialed by
              the builder and the homeowner, no witness needed; ...
              [line: notice:AL-insurance]

  ACTION      Check the pool safety rules in Alabama for this job (details)
              Portuguese (draft): Confira as regras de segurança de piscina em Alabama para este serviço
              Who does it: PERSON ONLY
              Old card text: Pool job: the Florida pool documents are not used in Alabama. The research lists:
              local code and permit clause.
              UNSURE: see general note 5 at the top.
              [line: pool]

  ACTION      Take dated photos before work starts and get the condition acknowledgment signed (good practice)
              Portuguese (draft): Tire fotos com data antes de começar e pegue a assinatura no termo de condição do imóvel
              Who does it: THE SYSTEM CAN SEE IT. On the card it reads: Dated photos and the signed condition acknowledgment / Fotos com data e o termo de condição do imóvel assinado
              Old card text: Take dated photos before work starts and have the customer sign the condition
              acknowledgment.
              [line: fixed:1]

  ACTION      Send the customer the complete signed copy as soon as both of you have signed (good practice)
              Portuguese (draft): Envie ao cliente a cópia completa e assinada assim que os dois tiverem assinado
              Who does it: THE SYSTEM DOES IT. On the card it reads: Complete signed copy for the customer / Cópia completa e assinada para o cliente
              Old card text: Give the customer a complete signed copy at signing.
              [line: fixed:2]

  ACTION      This plan renews by itself: check whether Alabama has an automatic renewal rule (details)
              Portuguese (draft): Este plano renova sozinho: confira se Alabama tem regra de renovação automática
              Who does it: PERSON ONLY
              Old card text: Automatic renewal rule: none found in the research. That is not proof that none
              exists.
              UNSURE: see general note 6 at the top.
              [line: cleaning_auto_renewal, cleaning agreements only]

  ACTION      Check whether the city or county requires a license for cleaning (details)
              Portuguese (draft): Confira se a cidade ou o condado exige licença para limpeza
              Who does it: PERSON ONLY
              Old card text: Cleaning licence: no note in the research; not verified. Check your city and
              county.
              UNSURE: see general note 7 at the top.
              [line: cleaning_license, cleaning agreements only]

  ACTION      Ask your tax professional whether to charge sales tax on this job (details)
              Portuguese (draft): Pergunte ao seu contador se deve cobrar imposto sobre vendas neste serviço
              Who does it: PERSON ONLY
              Old card text: Sales tax: the clause library holds Florida's rules only. This agreement prints
              no tax sentence for Alabama. Ask your tax professional.
              UNSURE: see general note 8 at the top.
              [line: cleaning_tax, cleaning agreements only]

  HANDLED     This builder is the written contract. [written_contract]
              Old card text: Written contract: Written contract with license number for licensees; no separate
              threshold. The research gives no clear dollar amount; use a written contract at any ...
  HANDLED     The contract states the right to cancel and counts the deadline. [cancellation]
              Old card text: Cancellation: Federal rule for cash sales; Ala. Code 5-19-12 for credit sales
              solicited away from the seller's place of business, until midnight of the third ...
  HANDLED     The builder computes the deadline and prints the federal notice and form.
              [cancellation_deadline]
              Old card text: The deadline this contract computes when it is sold at the customer's home: 3
              business days after the customer signs; Saturday counts; Sundays and federal holidays ...
  HANDLED     The federal cancellation notice and form print in the contract, and the research says this state
              accepts them instead. [notice:AL-cancel-credit]
              Old card text: Alabama requires this notice: BUYER'S RIGHT TO CANCEL (credit sales only) (Ala.
              Code 5-19-12). The exact wording is not loaded yet. Get it from the official source ...
  HANDLED     The contract prints the license or registration line. [item:1]
              Old card text: From the research for Alabama: "HBLB #" line.
  HANDLED     The contract is signed by both sides in the app. [item:3]
              Old card text: From the research for Alabama: Written contract with offer and acceptance signed
              by all.
  HANDLED     The builder lists every field that is still empty before you send. [fixed:3]
              Old card text: Leave no blank spaces in the contract.
              UNSURE: see general note 9 at the top.
  HANDLED     The builder leaves the Florida cleaning notices out and prints the federal ones when they apply.
              [cleaning_blocks, cleaning agreements only]
              Old card text: This agreement is in Alabama: the Florida cleaning notices (continuing-services
              notice, Florida home solicitation statement, Florida automatic-renewal panel, Florida ...

  BACKGROUND  How the state counts the days. The builder does the counting. [cancellation_days]
              Old card text: Until midnight of the third business day following execution by the buyer; if the
              seller has not given the statement the buyer may cancel within one year after the ...
  BACKGROUND  What the research found about deposit limits. When the limit is a clean number the builder
              compares your first payment with it. [deposit]
              Old card text: Deposit cap: None found.
  BACKGROUND  How this state handles construction defect claims. Where the research gives a cite, the warranty
              and dispute clauses already point to it. [defect]
              Old card text: Defect process: none found in the research (not proof that none exists).
  BACKGROUND  Says the same thing as another line on this list. [item:2]
              Old card text: From the research for Alabama: Written liability insurance disclosure before work
              starts (34-14A-19): insured builder, signed or initialed by builder and homeowner; ...
  BACKGROUND  How far the research for this state got. [status]
              Old card text: Research status for Alabama: notice text still needs official copy (1 of 2
              notices still have no official wording loaded (1 held for a problem named in the state ...
  BACKGROUND  Whether the state's door-to-door rule reaches cleaning. [cleaning_home_solicitation, cleaning
              agreements only]
              Old card text: Home-solicitation cancellation: the research did not find that Alabama's rule
              reaches cleaning services. That is not proof that it does not.

              Who does it: THE SYSTEM DOES IT. On the card it reads: "Notice of Potential Claims Must Be Provided within One Year" page, signed by the customer / Página "Notice of Potential Claims Must Be Provided within One Year", assinada pelo cliente
              Asked first: "Is this a new home, or a remodel that costs more than half of the home's assessed
              value?" No takes this line off the card. Yes, Not sure or no answer keeps it.
              Old card text: Alaska requires this: Notice of Potential Claims Must Be Provided within One Year
              (separate signed page attached to the contract) (AS 09.45.893). The official wording is on file.
              When: Contract to design, construct or remodel a dwelling (single-family house, duplex or
              multi-family unit); remodel means a change worth more than 25 percent of the structure's value.

  HANDLED     The contract's license line already uses the word this state uses. [license_word]
              Old card text: In Alaska say "registered", never "licensed".
  HANDLED     This builder is the written contract. [written_contract]
              Old card text: Written contract: None found. The research gives no clear dollar amount; use a

  HANDLED     The contract states the right to cancel and counts the deadline. [cancellation]

  HANDLED     The builder computes the deadline and prints the federal notice and form.
              [cancellation_deadline]
              Old card text: The deadline this contract computes when it is sold at the customer's home: 5
              business days after the customer signs; Saturday counts; Sundays and federal holidays ...
  HANDLED     The contract's cancellation notice already counts five business days for Alaska.
              [notice:AK-cancel]
              Old card text: Alaska requires this notice: Written notice of the five business day right to
              revoke (AS 45.02.350). The exact wording is not loaded yet. Get it from the official ...
              UNSURE: The statute gives no wording of its own. Marked handled because the builder prints the
              notice with the five-day count; a lawyer should confirm that is enough.
  HANDLED     The contract prints the license or registration line. [item:1]
              Old card text: From the research for Alaska: Registration number, name, mailing and principal
              address on contract and ads (AS 08.18.051, official text).
  HANDLED     The builder lists every field that is still empty before you send. [fixed:3]
              Old card text: Leave no blank spaces in the contract.
              UNSURE: see general note 9 at the top.
  HANDLED     The builder leaves the Florida cleaning notices out and prints the federal ones when they apply.
              [cleaning_blocks, cleaning agreements only]
              Old card text: This agreement is in Alaska: the Florida cleaning notices (continuing-services
              notice, Florida home solicitation statement, Florida automatic-renewal panel, Florida ...

  BACKGROUND  How the state counts the days. The builder does the counting. [cancellation_days]
              Old card text: Five business days from entering into the contract; the statute does not define
              business day. The buyer revokes by tendering the goods back or by registered letter ...
  BACKGROUND  What the research found about deposit limits. When the limit is a clean number the builder
              compares your first payment with it. [deposit]
              Old card text: Deposit cap: None found.
  BACKGROUND  How this state handles construction defect claims. Where the research gives a cite, the warranty
              and dispute clauses already point to it. [defect]
              Old card text: Defect process: none found in the research (not proof that none exists).
  BACKGROUND  Says the same thing as another line on this list. [item:2]
              Old card text: From the research for Alaska: Defect notice page (AS 09.45.893) titled "Notice of
              Potential Claims Must Be Provided within One Year", signed by the purchaser, ...
  BACKGROUND  Says the same thing as another line on this list. [item:3]
              Old card text: From the research for Alaska: Written notice of five business day revoke right.
  BACKGROUND  How far the research for this state got. [status]
              Old card text: Research status for Alaska: notice text still needs official copy (1 of 2 notices
              still have no official wording loaded (1 held for a problem named in the state ...
  BACKGROUND  Whether the state's door-to-door rule reaches cleaning. [cleaning_home_solicitation, cleaning
              agreements only]
              Old card text: Home-solicitation cancellation: the research says Alaska's rule reaches cleaning
              services.

On the card: 15 action lines at most. Under "More about Arizona": 14 lines.

              Old card text: License or registration: ROC license for work of $1,000 or more (A.R.S. 32-1121
              exemption under $1,000, "not a licensed contractor" in ads).
              UNSURE: see general note 1 at the top.
              [line: license]

  ACTION      Add your license or registration number in the document settings (A.R.S. 32-1121)
              Portuguese (draft): Cadastre o número da sua licença ou registro nas configurações dos documentos
              Who does it: THE SYSTEM CAN SEE IT. On the card it reads: Your license or registration number printed in the contract / O número da sua licença ou registro impresso no contrato
              Old card text: No license or registration number is on file. The contract prints "not provided".
              Add the number in the document settings.
              [line: license_number]

  ACTION      Give the contract and the cancellation notice in the language you used to make the sale (A.R.S. 44-5004)
              Portuguese (draft): Entregue o contrato e o aviso de cancelamento no idioma usado na venda
              Who does it: THE SYSTEM DOES IT. On the card it reads: Contract and cancellation notice in the language of the sale / Contrato e aviso de cancelamento no idioma da venda
              Asked first: "Was the sale made in English?" Yes: the system does it. No or Not sure: a person
              must give a translation (hand tick).
              Old card text: Give the contract and the cancellation notice in the language used in the sale.
              UNSURE: see general note 3 at the top.
              [line: cancellation_language]

  ACTION      Attach the Registrar of Contractors complaint statement (10 point bold, with phone and website) (A.R.S. 32-1158(A)(9))
              Portuguese (draft): Anexe a declaração de reclamação do Registrar of Contractors (negrito, corpo 10, com telefone e site)
              Who does it: PERSON ONLY
              Old card text: Arizona requires this notice: Registrar complaint statement (A.R.S.
              32-1158(A)(9)). The exact wording is not loaded yet. Get it from the official source or your
              attorney and attach it before the customer signs. When: Contracts over $1,000. Format: 10 point
              bold; with phone, website and the 32-1162 time limit.
              [line: notice:AZ-registrar]

  ACTION      Hand the customer the state pool safety notice (A.R.S. 36-1681(E))
              Portuguese (draft): Entregue ao cliente o aviso estadual de segurança de piscina
              Who does it: PERSON ONLY

              36-1681(E)). The exact wording is not loaded yet. Get it from the official source or your
              attorney and attach it before the customer signs. When: Agreement to build a swimming pool or
              contained body of water. Format: A notice explaining safety education and responsibilities of
              pool ownership as approved by the Department of Health Services.
              [line: notice:AZ-pool-notice]

  ACTION      If the customer signs a promissory note, put the required statement on the face of the note (A.R.S. 44-5005(B))
              Portuguese (draft): Se o cliente assinar uma nota promissória, coloque a declaração exigida na frente da nota
              Who does it: PERSON ONLY
              Asked first: "Is the customer signing a promissory note?" No takes this line off the card. Yes,
              Not sure or no answer keeps it.
              Old card text: Arizona requires this notice: Statement on the face of any note given in a home
              solicitation sale (A.R.S. 44-5005(B)). The exact wording is not loaded yet. Get it from the
              official source or your attorney and attach it before the customer signs. When: Each note or

              UNSURE: Applies only when a note is taken.

  ACTION      If you are selling a newly built home, add the Registrar complaint provision and get the buyer's initials (A.R.S. 12-1365(A))

              [line: license_local]

  ACTION      If the customer pays on credit, attach the state's credit-sale cancellation notice before they sign (407.710)
              Portuguese (draft): Se o cliente pagar a crédito, anexe o aviso de cancelamento de venda a crédito do estado antes de ele assinar
              Who does it: THE SYSTEM DOES IT. On the card it reads: NOTICE OF CANCELLATION statement printed in the contract (10 point bold), with the transaction date and your name and address filled in / Declaração NOTICE OF CANCELLATION impressa no contrato (negrito, corpo 10), com a data da transação e o seu nome e endereço preenchidos
              Asked first: "Is the customer paying on credit (in installments or with a finance charge)?" No
              takes this line off the card. Yes, Not sure or no answer keeps it.
              Old card text: Missouri requires this: BUYER'S RIGHT TO CANCEL (credit sales only) (407.710).
              The official wording is on file. When: Consumer credit sale solicited and signed at the
              residence; a cash sale is outside the statute (federal rule only). Format: One statement headed
              NOTICE OF CANCELLATION in 10-point boldface with the date of the transaction and the seller's
              name and mailing address; no duplicate tear-off form.
              UNSURE: The Revisor's page lays the notice out in a table; only the paragraph the buyer reads is
              cut, the caption is printed as its heading and the date above it. Compare with the rendered
              page; a lawyer should confirm.
              [line: notice:MO-cancel-credit]

  ACTION      If you want your subcontractors and suppliers to keep lien rights, get the CONSENT OF OWNER signed (RSMo 429.013(2))
              Portuguese (draft): Se quiser que subempreiteiros e fornecedores mantenham o direito de gravame, pegue o CONSENT OF OWNER assinado pelo proprietário
              Who does it: THE SYSTEM DOES IT. On the card it reads: CONSENT OF OWNER printed in the contract (10 point bold) and signed by the owner on its own / CONSENT OF OWNER impresso no contrato (negrito, corpo 10) e assinado à parte pelo proprietário
              Asked first: "Do you want your subcontractors and suppliers to keep lien rights on this job?" No
              takes this line off the card. Yes, Not sure or no answer keeps it.
              Old card text: Missouri requires this: CONSENT OF OWNER (lets subcontractors and suppliers file
              mechanic's liens) (RSMo 429.013(2)). The official wording is on file. When: Repair, remodeling
              or addition on owner-occupied residential property of four units or less, if the contractor
              wants its subcontractors and suppliers to have lien rights. Format: Ten point bold type, signed
              separately from the 429.012 notice.
              [line: notice:MO-consent]

              [line: cancellation_window]

  ACTION      Send the owner the NOTICE TO OWNER with a lien waiver form before you take any payment, including the deposit (W.S. 29-10-101(a); 29-2-112)
              Portuguese (draft): Envie ao proprietário o NOTICE TO OWNER com um formulário de lien waiver antes de receber qualquer pagamento, inclusive o sinal
              Who does it: THE SYSTEM DOES IT. On the card it reads: NOTICE TO OWNER, filled in and signed by the company, given to the owner with the lien waiver form / NOTICE TO OWNER, preenchido e assinado pela empresa, entregue ao proprietário com o formulário de lien waiver
              Old card text: Wyoming requires this: NOTICE TO OWNER (preliminary notice of right to lien)
              (W.S. 29-10-101(a); 29-2-112). The official wording is on file. When: Contractor, before
              receiving any payment including advances; sent to the record owner, with a lien waiver form
              attached. Format: Substantially the statutory form; no type size; the notice says a lien waiver
              form is attached.
              UNSURE: The owner gets the notice with the contract, so before any payment made after the
              contract is sent. A payment taken before the contract is sent is outside what the system sees.
              [line: notice:WY-lien]
